- **selection-set-limits**: `selection set-limits` changes the parsing limits on the project's current Default Selection. The chosen Texts, added words, name, and setup status stay in place. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/selection-set-limits.md) · ★1)
- **selection-set-parsimony**: `selection set-parsimony` saves the Texts that Parsimony's Text group reads. Every Parsimony run reuses the set until you change it, so the evidence for judging the grammar stays the same from run to run. ([sillsdev/motif](https://github.com/sillsdev/motif/blob/HEAD/src/SIL.Motif.Help/Content/en/commands/selection-set-parsimony.md) · ★1)

- **selection.by-attribute**: Select every object whose user-text attribute matches a value (or regex). The "find every part tagged X" / "select by mark" primitive — pairs naturally with `user-text.bulk-write`. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/rhino-8/commands/selection.by-attribute.md) · ★7)
- **selection.by-attribute**: Select entities whose AttributeDictionary value matches an exact value or regex. The SketchUp analog of Rhino's `selection.by-attribute`. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/architecture/sketchup-2026/commands/selection.by-attribute.md) · ★7 · 2 copies)
- **selection.by-property**: Find every `ModelItem` whose property matches a value — the coordination-side equivalent of Revit's `element.by-parameter-value`. Read-only. ([aware-aeco/aware](https://github.com/aware-aeco/aware/blob/HEAD/20-agents/aeco/construction/navisworks/commands/selection.by-property.md) · ★7)
- **selective-phase-run**: 1. **Identify Target Phase** - Accept the phase argument: `{phase_name}` - Map to todo range: - `foundation` → foundation-01 through foundation-15 - `wiring` → wiring-01 through wiring-15 - `implementation` → impl-01 through impl-20 - `validation` → validation-01 through validation-15 - `automation` → automation-01 through automation-15 - `intelligence` → intelligence-01 through intelligence-15 - ([cdalsoniii/brightpath-coder](https://github.com/cdalsoniii/brightpath-coder/blob/HEAD/.cursor/commands/selective-phase-run.md))
- **selectoneless**: Deselects one object and leaves the rest selected. It removes the most recently selected object that is not kept at the front of the selection order. When every selected object is kept at the front, it removes the one that was selected first. If the view is following the removed object, it stops following.
